=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
An election is being held with N candidates numbered 1, 2, \ldots, N. There are K votes, some of which have been counted so far.
Up until now, candidate i has received A_i votes.
After all ballots are counted, candidate i (1 \leq i \leq N) will be elected if and only if the number of candidates who have received more votes than them is less than M.  There may be multiple candidates elected.
For each candidate, find the minimum number of additional votes they need from the remaining ballots to guarantee their victory regardless of how the other candidates receive votes.
Formally, solve the following problem for each i = 1,2,\ldots,N.
Determine if there is a non-negative integer X not exceeding K - \displaystyle{\sum_{i=1}^{N}} A_i satisfying the following condition.  If it exists, find the minimum possible such integer.

- If candidate i receives X additional votes, then candidate i will always be elected.

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N M K
A_1 A_2 \ldots A_N

Output

Let C_i be the minimum number of additional votes candidate i needs from the remaining ballots to guarantee their victory regardless of how other candidates receive votes. Print C_1, C_2, \ldots, C_N separated by spaces.
If candidate i has already secured their victory, then let C_i = 0. If candidate i cannot secure their victory under any circumstances, then let C_i = -1.

Constraints


- 1 \leq M \leq N \leq 2 \times 10^5
- 1 \leq K \leq 10^{12}
- 0 \leq A_i \leq 10^{12}
- \displaystyle{\sum_{i=1}^{N} A_i} \leq K
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

5 2 16
3 1 4 1 5

Sample Output 1

2 -1 1 -1 0

14 votes have been counted so far, and 2 votes are left.
The C to output is (2, -1, 1, -1, 0).  For example:

- Candidate 1 can secure their victory by obtaining 2 more votes, while not by obtaining 1 more vote.  Thus, C_1 = 2.
- Candidate 2 can never (even if they obtain 2 more votes) secure their victory, so C_2 = -1.

Sample Input 2

12 1 570
81 62 17 5 5 86 15 7 79 26 6 28

Sample Output 2

79 89 111 117 117 74 112 116 80 107 117 106

Use the following starter code to write the final solution.
```python

```

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

Candidate i final votes = A_i + X. Let S_i = A_i + X. Candidate i is elected iff fewer than M candidates have final votes > S_i. Since ties not count as more. So adversary wants to make at least M candidates have votes > S_i. They have total votes T = R - X to allocate among N-1 other candidates (can give any nonnegative integer). Candidate i's own final votes fixed. Need determine if adversary can allocate T among N-1 candidates so that at least M candidates end with > S_i. Since only need M candidates > S_i, and other candidates can get zero. To make a candidate j have > S_i, need give at least S_i - A_j + 1 votes (if A_j <= S_i; if already A_j > S_i, then already > S_i, need 0 additional). Let need_j(i, X) = max(0, A_i + X - A_j + 1) for j != i. Candidate i is guaranteed iff the sum of the M smallest such needs among j != i is > T? Wait adversary can choose any M candidates to exceed. To make at least M candidates exceed, minimum total votes required is sum of M smallest needs among all other candidates. If T >= that minimum, adversary can make i not elected. If T < minimum, impossible. Thus guarantee condition:
T < min over subset U of other candidates, |U|=M, sum_{j in U} max(0, S_i - A_j + 1).
Equivalently F_i(X) = sum of M smallest values max(0, A_i+X - A_j +1) over j != i. Need F_i(X) > R - X. If true, i guaranteed with X. Need minimal X in [0,R] satisfying, else -1. Is this correct? Let's test sample1.

Function ok(i,x):
s = A[i] + x
p = bisect_right(vals, s)  # number candidates with A <= s
L = M + p - N  # could be negative; if L <=0 return False? G=0, R-x>=0, so false. But if L<0, G=0. If L=0, G=0, false. So return False if L<=0.
But wait if L=0, G=0; condition 0 > R-x false since R-x >=0. So false. If L<0 also false. So need L>0.
Then sum_top_L_all = pref[p] - pref[p-L] (since L<=p? Need ensure L <= p. L = M + p - N. Since M<=N, L<=p. If L>0 yes.)
r = pos[i] (1-based sorted position). Need exclude i if r in [p-L+1, p].
If r in block:
    sum_excl = sum_top_L_all - vals[r-1] + (vals[p-L-1] if p-L >=1 else 0)
else:
    sum_excl = sum_top_L_all
G = L*(s+1) - sum_excl
return G > R - x

Let C = A_i + x + 1. Let R_i_rhs = R + A_i + 1. Inequality:
G > R - x = R - (C - A_i -1) = R + A_i + 1 - C = RHS - C.
So:
H_T(C) + delta_i > RHS - C
=> H_T(C) + C > RHS - delta_i
where delta_i = A_i - b if i in T else 0.
Equivalently H_T(C) + C > R + A_i + 1 - delta_i.

Since H_T(C) is nondecreasing in C, and C + H_T(C) is strictly increasing? H_T(C) = sum_{a in T} max(0,C-a). Its derivative (slope) = count of a < C, between 0 and M. Pseudo:

def build_segments(T_asc, M):
    pref = [0]*(M+1)
    for i,a in enumerate(T_asc):
        pref[i+1] = pref[i]+a
    seg_k = []
    seg_end = []
    INF = 10**30
    # initial k=0
    l=1
    r=T_asc[0]
    if l <= r:
        seg_k.append(0)
        seg_end.append(r)  # F_end = r
    i=0
    while i < M:
        v = T_asc[i]
        j=i
        while j < M and T_asc[j] == v:
            j += 1
        k = j  # count <= v
        l = v + 1
        if j < M:
            r = T_asc[j]  # next distinct value
        else:
            r = INF
        if l <= r:
            seg_k.append(k)
            seg_end.append((k+1)*r - pref[k])
        i = j
    return pref, seg_k, seg_end

But wait: What about if initial segment skipped because T_asc[0]=0, then after v=0, l=1, r=next_v. Good. If T_asc[0] > 1, initial segment [1, T_asc[0]] k=0. Then after v=T_asc[0], l=v+1, r=next_v. Good. If T_asc[0]=1, initial [1,1] k=0. After v=1, l=2, r=next_v. At C=2, k=count<=1. Good.

Need ensure segment ranges cover C=1 when T_asc[0]=0. Initial skipped, after v=0 l=1. Good.

Now, F_end for initial segment: F(C)=C, at r=T_asc[0], F_end=r. If r maybe 0? skipped. Good.

Now, inverse_F(Y):
idx = bisect_right(seg_end, Y)  # first end > Y
# Because seg_end sorted increasing. Need if idx == len(seg_end)? Should not for finite Y due INF, but if Y >= INF? no.
k = seg_k[idx]
num = Y + pref[k]
C0 = num // (k+1) + 1
# optional clamp to segment l? We didn't store l. Could store seg_l for safety.
return C0

But wait: bisect_right returns first index where seg_end > Y. If seg_end contains INF, and Y < INF, idx < len. Good.

Need confirm seg_end strictly increasing. Let's prove with duplicates. Segment0 end = v0 (if v0>=1). Segment after v0 end = next_v (or INF), F_end = (c0+1)*next_v - pref[c0]. Since next_v > v0, and F at next_v = actual F(next_v). Actual F(next_v) = H_T(next_v)+next_v. H_T(next_v) = sum_{a < next_v}(next_v-a) = sum_{a<=v0}(next_v-a) = c0*next_v - pref[c0]. So F_end = (c0+1)next_v - pref[c0]. This is > F(v0)=v0? Since next_v > v0 and c0>=0, yes. If initial skipped v0=0, first segment after v0 end=next_v, F_end=(c0+1)next_v - pref[c0] >0. Good. Across segments, F_end increasing because F(C) increasing and segment ends increasing. Good.

Implementation plan:

Read N,M,K
A list length N
R = K - sum(A)

If M == N:
    print(' '.join(['0']*N)); return

# Sort A ascending
sorted_A = sorted(A)
T_asc = sorted_A[N-M:]  # M largest ascending
b = sorted_A[N-M-1]     # (M+1)-th largest

# Mark inT: top M candidates by A
# We can sort indices by A descending:
order = sorted(range(N), key=lambda i: A[i], reverse=True)
inT = [False]*N
for idx in order[:M]:
    inT[idx] = True

# Build pref, segments for T_asc
pref = [0]*(M+1)
for i,a in enumerate(T_asc):
    pref[i+1] = pref[i]+a

INF = 10**30
seg_k=[]
seg_end=[]
l=1; r=T_asc[0]
if l <= r:
    seg_k.append(0)
    seg_end.append(r)
i=0
while i < M:
    v=T_asc[i]
    j=i
    while j<M and T_asc[j]==v:
        j+=1
    k=j
    l=v+1
    if j<M:
        r=T_asc[j]
    else:
        r=INF
    if l <= r:
        seg_k.append(k)
        seg_end.append((k+1)*r - pref[k])
    i=j

from bisect import bisect_right

ans=[]
for idx,a in enumerate(A):
    L = a + 1
    if inT[idx]:
        delta = a - b
    else:
        delta = 0
    Y = R + a + 1 - delta
    pos = bisect_right(seg_end, Y)
    # pos should < len(seg_end)
    k = seg_k[pos]
    C0 = (Y + pref[k]) // (k+1) + 1
    C = C0 if C0 > L else L
    if C > L + R:
        ans.append(-1)
    else:
        ans.append(C - L)  # x = C - a -1 = C-L
print(' '.join(map(str, ans)))

Pseudo code final:

import sys
from bisect import bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.readline
    N,M,K = map(int,input().split())
    A = list(map(int,input().split()))
    R = K - sum(A)
    if M == N:
        print(' '.join(['0']*N))
        return

    sorted_A = sorted(A)
    T = sorted_A[N-M:]  # M largest, ascending
    b = sorted_A[N-M-1]  # (M+1)-th largest

    # mark candidates in T
    order = sorted(range(N), key=A.__getitem__, reverse=True)
    inT = [False]*N
    for idx in order[:M]:
        inT[idx] = True

    Mlen = M
    pref = [0]*(Mlen+1)
    for i,a in enumerate(T):
        pref[i+1] = pref[i] + a

    INF = 10**30
    seg_k = []
    seg_end = []

    # segment k=0: C <= T[0]
    l = 1
    r = T[0]
    if l <= r:
        seg_k.append(0)
        seg_end.append(r)  # F(r)=r

    i = 0
    while i < Mlen:
        v = T[i]
        j = i
        while j < Mlen and T[j] == v:
            j += 1
        k = j  # number of T elements <= v
        l = v + 1
        if j < Mlen:
            r = T[j]  # next distinct value
        else:
            r = INF
        if l <= r:
            seg_k.append(k)
            seg_end.append((k + 1) * r - pref[k])
        i = j

    ans = []
    for idx,a in enumerate(A):
        L = a + 1
        if inT[idx]:
            Y = R + b + 1
        else:
            Y = R + a + 1
        pos = bisect_right(seg_end, Y)
        k = seg_k[pos]
        C0 = (Y + pref[k]) // (k + 1) + 1
        C = C0 if C0 > L else L
        if C > L + R:
            ans.append(-1)
        else:
            ans.append(C - L)
    print(' '.join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Let's write final code carefully:

import sys
from bisect import bisect_right

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, M, K = data[0], data[1], data[2]
    A = data[3:3+N]
    R = K - sum(A)

    if M == N:
        print(' '.join(['0'] * N))
        return

    sorted_A = sorted(A)
    T = sorted_A[N-M:]          # M largest, ascending
    b = sorted_A[N-M-1]         # (M+1)-th largest

    order = sorted(range(N), key=A.__getitem__, reverse=True)
    inT = [False] * N
    for idx in order[:M]:
        inT[idx] = True
    # optionally del order

    pref = [0] * (M + 1)
    for i, v in enumerate(T):
        pref[i+1] = pref[i] + v

    INF = 10**30
    seg_k = []
    seg_end = []

    # C <= T[0]
    if 1 <= T[0]:
        seg_k.append(0)
        seg_end.append(T[0])   # F(T[0]) = T[0]

    i = 0
    while i < M:
        v = T[i]
        j = i
        while j < M and T[j] == v:
            j += 1
        k = j
        l = v + 1
        if j < M:
            r = T[j]
        else:
            r = INF
        if l <= r:
            seg_k.append(k)
            seg_end.append((k + 1) * r - pref[k])
        i = j

    ans = []
    for idx, a in enumerate(A):
        L = a + 1
        if inT[idx]:
            Y = R + b + 1
        else:
            Y = R + a + 1

        pos = bisect_right(seg_end, Y)
        k = seg_k[pos]
        C0 = (Y + pref[k]) // (k + 1) + 1
        C = C0 if C0 > L else L

        if C > L + R:
            ans.append(-1)
        else:
            ans.append(C - L)

    print(' '.join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
